<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Again `Terminated` with EXIT_CODE=143 (SIGTERM, 128+15). The process was killed at 24836/64043 (38%) after about 33 seconds of GPU inference. The screen session was also killed.

This looks like a systematic issue. The process is being killed every time it runs for about 30 seconds. This strongly suggests there's a server-side watchdog or resource management system that kills processes.

Let me check:
1. Is there a cgroup limit?
2. Is there a process monitoring service?
3. Is the issue related to memory consumption?

Looking at the timing: the process runs for about 30 seconds before being killed. 

The timing aligns with that SSH timeout, but we're using screen so that shouldn't matter. EXIT_CODE=143 means SIGTERM was sent explicitly—not an OOM kill. Something is actively terminating these processes, and I'm noticing there's another process running with a similar command that was started from a different session, which suggests multiple attempts are interfering with each other.

The real puzzle is why the screen session itself is dying—screen should survive SIGHUP, so something must be directly killing the screen process. Could be a process limit, job scheduler, or maybe the Hugging Face downloads are timing out and causing the whole thing to hang.

Actually, looking at the logs more carefully, the process was running GPU inference at 767 iterations per second and got killed after just 33 seconds—it wasn't downloading anything. Let me try running the benchmarks one task at a time instead of all six simultaneously, so each completes faster and hopefully avoids whatever's terminating the process.
```

   - **Task 1**: Check a previously-launched smoke test (Transformer baseline lambada_openai via nohup). If PPL < 50 → PASSED; if PPL > 1000 → FAILED, apply tie_weights fix and re-run.
   - **Task 2**: Run full benchmarks for 3 models (Transformer baseline, GatedDeltaNet hybrid-3-1, RetNet pure), each with 9 tasks (lambada_openai, hellaswag, arc_easy, arc_challenge, winogrande, openbookqa, logiqa2 as a batch; mmlu 5-shot separately; blimp separately). Order: Transformer → GatedDeltaNet → RetNet. Report a complete results table with gap analysis.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **lm-eval-harness** (v0.4.12): Evaluation framework for language models
   - **FLA (Flash Linear Attention)**: Library providing `TransformerForCausalLM`, `GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM`, `RetNetForCausalLM` model classes
   - **Weight tying**: `lm_head.weight` should share the same tensor as embedding weights. FLA models store `lm_head.weight` as MISSING in checkpoints (because `tie_word_embeddings=True`), relying on `tie_weights()` to restore it
   - **Broken `tie_weights()`**: FLA's `TransformerForCausalLM` uses `model.embeddings` path, but the base class `tie_weights()` looks for `model.embed_tokens`, so it silently fails
   - **Manual weight tying**: Direct assignment `model.lm_head.weight = model.model.embeddings.weight` works correctly
   - **Model type `transformer`**: Not registered in AutoConfig by default; requires manual registration via FLA's `TransformerConfig`
   - **transformers 5.8.1**, **datasets 4.8.5**, **torch 2.8.0+cu128**, **Python 3.12**
   - **Process killing issue**: Long-running nohup/setsid processes get SIGTERM (EXIT_CODE=143) after ~30s; screen sessions survive

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`/root/entrainment_gated_ft/scripts/run_lmeval_fla.py`**
     - Central evaluation script that registers FLA model types with AutoConfig/AutoModelForCausalLM and includes monkey-patch for weight tying
     - Modified to add `transformer` model type registration (the monkey-patch for from_pretrained was already present but wasn't being called)
     - Current content after modifications:
     ```python
     #!/usr/bin/env python3
     import functools
     from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoConfig
     
     try:
         from fla.models.transformer import TransformerConfig, TransformerForCausalLM
         AutoConfig.register('transformer', TransformerConfig)
         AutoModelForCausalLM.register(TransformerConfig, TransformerForCausalLM)
     except (ImportError, ValueError):
         pass
     try:
         from fla.models.gated_deltanet import GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM
         AutoConfig.register('gated_deltanet', GatedDeltaNetConfig)
         AutoModelForCausalLM.register(GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM)
     except (ImportError, ValueError):
         pass
     try:
         from fla.models.hgrn2 import HGRN2Config, HGRN2ForCausalLM
         AutoConfig.register('hgrn2', HGRN2Config)
         AutoModelForCausalLM.register(HGRN2Config, HGRN2ForCausalLM)
     except (ImportError, ValueError):
         pass
     try:
         from fla.models.retnet import RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM
         AutoConfig.register('retnet', RetNetConfig)
         AutoModelForCausalLM.register(RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM)
     except (ImportError, ValueError):
         pass
     
     _orig = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained
     
     @functools.wraps(_orig)
     def _patched(*a, **kw):
         m = _orig(*a, **kw)
         if hasattr(m, 'lm_head') and hasattr(m, 'model'):
             emb = getattr(m.model, 'embeddings', None) or getattr(m.model, 'embed_tokens', None)
             if emb is not None:
                 m.lm_head.weight = emb.weight
                 tied = m.lm_head.weight.data_ptr() == emb.weight.data_ptr()
                 print(f"Weight tying ({type(emb).__name__}): {'OK' if tied else 'FAILED'}")
             else:
                 print(f"WARNING: no embeddings or embed_tokens found on model.model")
         return m
     
     AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained = _patched
     
     from lm_eval.__main__ import cli_evaluate
     cli_evaluate()
     ```

   - **`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/lm_eval/models/huggingface.py`**
     - lm_eval's HFLM model class; patched in two places
     - Patch 1 (line 824): Added `self._model.tie_weights()` after `from_pretrained` call (less important since line 389 already does this)
     - Patch 2 (after line 389): Manual weight tying for FLA models - this is the critical fix
     ```python
     # Around line 389 (after self.model.tie_weights()):
             self.model.tie_weights()
             # Manual weight tying for FLA models
             _emb = getattr(self.model, "model", None)
             if _emb is not None:
                 _emb = getattr(_emb, "embeddings", None) or getattr(_emb, "embed_tokens", None)
             if _emb is not None and hasattr(self.model, "lm_head"):
                 self.model.lm_head.weight = _emb.weight
                 print(f"Manual weight tying: {self.model.lm_head.weight.data_ptr() == _emb.weight.data_ptr()}")
     ```

   - **`/tmp/run_all_benchmarks.sh`**
     - Master benchmark script running in screen session, processing all 3 models with all tasks sequentially
     - Uses `exec > /tmp/lmeval_all_benchmarks.log 2>&1` for output redirection
     - Runs 6 tasks individually, then mmlu 5-shot, then blimp for each model
     - Creates `/tmp/lmeval_all_benchmarks.done` on completion
     ```bash
     #!/bin/bash
     exec > /tmp/lmeval_all_benchmarks.log 2>&1
     source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
     source /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null
     export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com
     export HF_DATASETS_TRUST_REMOTE_CODE=1
     export PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
     
     MODELS=(
       "/root/autodl-tmp/models_1.3b/transformer_1.3B_baseline"
       "/root/autodl-tmp/models_1.3b/1.3B-100B-GatedDeltaNet-hybrid-3-1"
       "/root/autodl-tmp/models_1.3b/1.3B-100B-RetNet-pure"
     )
     TASKS=(lambada_openai hellaswag arc_easy arc_challenge winogrande openbookqa)
     
     for MODEL_PATH in "${MODELS[@]}"; do
       MODEL_NAME=$(basename "$MODEL_PATH")
       OUTPUT_DIR="/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results_1.3b/lm_eval_fixed/$MODEL_NAME"
       mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_DIR"
       echo "===== MODEL: $MODEL_NAME ====="
       for TASK in "${TASKS[@]}"; do
         echo "--- Task: $TASK ---"
         python3 /root/entrainment_gated_ft/scripts/run_lmeval_fla.py \
           --model hf \
           --model_args pretrained=$MODEL_PATH,trust_remote_code=True,dtype=bfloat16 \
           --tasks $TASK --batch_size 16 --output_path "$OUTPUT_DIR" --log_samples
         echo "--- $TASK done (exit=$?) ---"
       done
       echo "--- Task: mmlu (5-shot) ---"
       python3 /root/entrainment_gated_ft/scripts/run_lmeval_fla.py \
         --model hf \
         --model_args pretrained=$MODEL_PATH,trust_remote_code=True,dtype=bfloat16 \
         --tasks mmlu --num_fewshot 5 --batch_size 16 --output_path "$OUTPUT_DIR" --log_samples
       echo "--- mmlu done (exit=$?) ---"
       echo "--- Task: blimp ---"
       python3 /root/entrainment_gated_ft/scripts/run_lmeval_fla.py \
         --model hf \
         --model_args pretrained=$MODEL_PATH,trust_remote_code=True,dtype=bfloat16 \
         --tasks blimp --batch_size 16 --output_path "$OUTPUT_DIR" --log_samples
       echo "--- blimp done (exit=$?) ---"
       echo "===== $MODEL_NAME COMPLETE ====="
     done
     echo "ALL DONE"
     touch /tmp/lmeval_all_benchmarks.done
     ```

   - **Model configs**: `transformer_1.3B_baseline/config.json` has `"model_type": "transformer"`, `"tie_word_embeddings": true`, `"architectures": ["TransformerForCausalLM"]`, no `auto_map`

   - **Results directory**: `/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results_1.3b/lm_eval_fixed/&lt;model_name>/`

4. Errors and fixes:

   - **Smoke test PPL = 16,274,872 (FAILED)**:
     - Root cause: `lm_head.weight` randomly initialized; FLA's `tie_weights()` broken because it looks for `model.embed_tokens` but model uses `model.embeddings`
     - Fix: Manual weight tying in lm_eval source + transformer registration in run_lmeval_fla.py
     - Smoke test v4 PPL = 13.06 → PASSED

   - **AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained monkey-patch not being called**:
     - The "Weight tying" message never appeared in logs for early smoke tests
     - Root cause unclear (possibly classmethod binding issue), but adding the manual tying patch directly in lm_eval's HFLM `__init__` (line 389) bypassed the issue
     - After adding transformer registration, both patches now work (both messages appear)

   - **`logiqa2` dataset incompatibility with datasets 4.8.5**:
     - Error: `RuntimeError: Dataset scripts are no longer supported, but found logiqa2.py`
     - Tried downgrading datasets to 3.6.0 → new error: `ValueError: Feature type 'List' not found`
     - Restored datasets 4.8.5; removed logiqa2 from 6-task batch (user's script mentions logiqa2 but it's incompatible)
     - NOTE: `logiqa` (v1) task exists as alternative, not yet tested

   - **Processes killed by SIGTERM (EXIT_CODE=143)**:
     - When running 6+ tasks together (~64K requests), process killed after ~30s of GPU inference
     - Tried nohup → killed; setsid+nohup → killed; screen → WORKS
     - Fix: Run tasks one-by-one in a screen session instead of all together
     - Killed orphan process 215664 that was using GPU memory from a previous run

   - **pyc cache issue**: Cleared `__pycache__/huggingface*` files after patching lm_eval source to ensure patches take effect

   - **PARTIALLY SOLVED**: logiqa2 incompatibility - removed from batch; could try `logiqa` v1 as alternative
